Faculty of Information Science and Technology
Discover UKM
Give2UKM
Faculty Background
The Faculty of Information Science and Technology (Fakulti Teknologi dan Sains Maklumat) (FTSM) has established its academic roots as early as the conception of Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia (UKM). Before FTSM ever became a full-fledged faculty of its own, courses related to Computer Science have long been offered by UKM since the university was established in 1970. In line with the emergence of the computer science discipline, UKM set up the Statistics and Computer Science Unit in 1977 under the Quantitative School. The Statistics and Computer Science Unit was upgraded to become the Computer Science Department in 1982 while the Quantitative School became the Faculty of Mathematics and Computer Science in 1991. Academic programs at the Undergraduate, Master and Doctor of Philosophy (PhD) levels have been introduced incrementally. The number of academic staff with PhDs increased in accordance with the national requirements related to enhancing the quality of teaching and research.
In the 1990s, the world economy was driven by knowledge-based economics which was in tandem with the emergence of Information and Communication Technology (ICT). Not to be left out of these exciting developments, Malaysia devised various policies and initiatives including the Multimedia Super Corridor(MSC). Subsequently, ICT has become one of the nation’s most important industries. In line with the changes that have moved the world from the industrial to the information era, the Computer Science Department was upgraded on 1 October 1994 to become the Faculty of Information Science and Technology (Fakulti Teknologi dan Sains Maklumat) (FTSM). Strengthened by 40 academic staff, the faculty was awarded the ‘MSC company’ status on 24th November 2000.
Today, the FTSM family consists of a staff of 138 members. Academics, research and consultancy activities are carried out by 90 faculty members that includes 8 professors, 26 associate professors, 48 senior lecturers, 2 lecturers and 6 IT Instructors. The Faculty is also managed with the support of 25 administrative staff and 23 ICT support staff. The FTSM campus is located at the UKM Main Campus in Bangi. It also houses 3 research centers which are driven by experts in various fields of research. Since its inception, FTSM has progressed rapidly in various aspects. FTSM students too, are making their presence felt by being actively involved in numerous programs and competitions at the national and international levels.
At present, research in FTSM is streamlined into 3 focus areas for innovation, guided by experts at the 3 research centers hosted within FTSM: the Center for Artificial Intelligence Technology (CAIT), the Center for Cyber Security (CYBER), and the Center for Software Technology and Management (SOFTAM). Between 2019 and 2022, these 3 research centers collectively managed RM18,029,703.30 in research grants and funding and have collectively produced 154 innovations, published 644 journal articles and 47 books within these 3 focus areas.
In recognition of its many academic and research achievements since its establishment, FTSM was awarded the highly coveted status of
“Premier Digital Tech Institution”
by the Malaysian Digital Economy Corporation (MDEC) on 18 December 2023.
Three Decades of FTSM
Timeline
1977
Computer Science programme was established in UKM
1982
Upgraded to The Department of Computer Science
1991
Faculty of Computer Science and Mathematics was established
1994
Faculty of Information Science and Technology was established
2000
FTSM successfully acquired MSC status company by MDEC
2006
Center For Artificial Intelligence Technology (CAIT) was established
2010
Center For Software Technology & Management (SOFTAM) was established
2017
Center For Cyber Security (CYBER) was established
2023
FTSM was recognized as Premier Digital Technology Institution (PDTI) by MDEC

Mission, Vision & Objective
Mission
The Faculty of Information Science and Technology pledges to be a center of excellence in learning, teaching, research, consultancy and as a reference in the field of information science and technology based on the philosophy, vision and mission of UKM.
Vision
Be a selected faculty to produce educated talent to fill the needs of the country in the field of information technology.
Objective
To produce competent graduates in the field of information science and technology.
To be a centre for research and development in the field of information science and technology.
To provide consultancy and reference services in the field of information science and technology.
To disseminate knowledge of information science and technology to society.
To promote interaction and cohesion of knowledge between members in information science and technology.

Vision
Regional Quality Leader With Global Perspective.
Mission
Quality Assurance of Core Processes To Produce Value Added Service Delivery, Customer Satisfaction and Organisation Satisfaction.
Objective
Coordinate the implementation of UKM Quality Life cycle and comprehensive continuous improvement to foster a culture of quality among its citizens
Ensure quality and recognition of study programmes through audit and benchmarking
Ensure the Quality Management System is internalised by the citizens of UKM
Improve the quality of core processes and service delivery through the development of internal standard, customer feedback and stakeholder systems

Faculty Map
The Faculty of Information Science and Technology (FTSM) is located near to the Faculty of Engineering and Built Environment in Bangi Campus, Selangor. You may come here via the First Link entrance by using the Kuala Lumpur – Seremban Highway and exiting through the Kajang toll, or may also exit through the Bandar Baru Bangi toll. For those who are coming from Sepang, you may take the Second Link entrance and head directly to FTSM. Among the public transportation available to for your convenience are:
KTM Komuter - towards Seremban. Kindly depart at UKM Station. Take a taxi or a RapidKL Bus No.430 to FTSM
RapidKL Bus – take the No.E2 bus to Kajang Terminal. Once arrive, switch to RapidKL Bus No. 430 to FTSM
KLIA Limousine Service – from Kuala Lumpur International Airport (KLIA)
KLIA Express Transit – stop at Bandar Tasik Selatan Station, take the KTM Komuter towards Seremban and stop at UKM Station. Then, take a taxi or RapidKL Bus No.430 to FTSM

INTERNATIONAL MOBILITY PROGRAMMES
A unique opportunity for students to combine an overseas travel and cultural experience
FLEXIBLE LEARNING
Open Learning & Blended Learning
FLEXIBLE INDUSTRIAL READINESS PROGRAMMES
Partnership with the industry to bring in real-world case studies, skills and knowledge
The Faculty of Information Science and Information Technology is a stimulating environment in which new ideas, experimental designs, and robust systems are plentiful.
Our teaching programmes are always among the top in Malaysia. We also enjoy strong links with industries, which helps to ensure that our research and teaching is well informed and relevant to the needs of society.
Life for undergraduate majors centers around the various educational computing facility which is housed in the faculty. Apart from the major academic courses, all undergraduate students will participate in various Special Interest Groups (SIGs), aiming to promote students in applying and fostering their skills dan knowledge to industries and communities.
These SIGs ranging from Intelligent Machines Club, Cyber Hack & Ethics to Video Innovation inculcate creativities and innovations of our students. In the final year of the four years of the undergraduate programs, students will embark on a final year project and five months of industrial attachment.
Life for postgraduate students will mainly focus on the three research centers within the faculty: the Center for Artificial Intelligence (CAIT), the Center for Software Technology & Management (SOFTAM), and the Center for Cyber Security (Siber). Students will belong to either of these research centers which support conducive and supportive research environments.

PROSPECTUS
STPM / KPM Matriculation / Foundation
1. A pass in Matriculation or Foundation studies or Sijil Tinggi Persekolahan Malaysia (STPM) with minimum CGPA of 2.50 and least grade C (NGMP 2.00) in any two of the following subjects:
Biology
Physics / Engineering Physics
Chemistry / Engineering Chemistry
Information and Communications Technology (ICT) / Computer Science
Mathematics / Mathematics M / Mathematics T
AND
2. At least Grade C/Grade 6C in Mathematics or Additional Mathematics in SPM level
AND
Any one of the following subjects :
Biology
Physics
Chemistry
Science
Engineering Drawing
Mechanical Engineering Studies
Civil Engineering Studies
Electrical and Electronic Engineering Studies
Invention
Engineering Technology
Information and Communication Technology
Computer Science
Technical Communication Graphics
Additional Science
Applied Science
Multimedia Production
Computer Graphic
Construction Technology
Principles of Electrical and Electronics
Applied Electrical and Electronics
Computerized Machining
Computer Applications in Business
3. AND At least Band 3 in Malaysian University English Test (MUET)
4. AND Pass the test and/or interview as determined by the Faculty
Diploma Holders
A Diploma in Computing or equivalent from any educational institutions certified by the University and Government with a minimum CGPA of 3.00, AND
Acquired at least Grade C / Grade 6C in Mathematics in SPM level, AND
At least Grade C in Mathematics at Diploma level, AND
Applicants are required to provide the academic transcript from Semester 1 until the final semester, AND
At least Band 3 in Malaysian University English Test (MUET), AND
Pass the test and/or interview determined by the Faculty.
Private and International Track
General Requirements:
A Senior High School / Senior Secondary School / Other Certificates from government schools (with a period of at least 11 to 12 years of study from Primary to Higher Secondary), OR
GCE ‘A’ Level examination obtained at one sitting, OR
A Pass in Diploma / STPM or other certificate that is recognized by the Senate of the University and Malaysian Government as equivalent to the above
Applicants must have good grades with an overall average of 80% including 80% or more in Mathematics and two (2) Science subjects and English Languages
Language Requirements:
The applicants need to pass either:
Test of English as a Foreign Language (TOEFL) with a minimum score of 500, OR
International English Language Testing System (IELTS) with at least band 5.0, OR
UKM English Proficiency Placement Test (EPPT) with at least Band 3

Center for Academic Management UKM (Pusat Pengurusan Akademik)
Applications for full time study programmes for STPM, Matriculation, STAM and Diploma / Equivalent must be submitted via Ministry of Higher Education Malaysia’s Online Application. Applications for studies in all the programmes offered are published in local newspapers between the month of December and February every year.
Advertisements for the Second Channel intake (full time programmes) are published in November every year and applications for this must be submitted via UKM Online Application.
Non-citizen and International Student candidates who are interested in enrolling for the full time study programmes must submit their applications via UKM
Online Application open in January every year.
International undergraduate students are managed by specially designed programmes between the governments. Currently there is an allocation of only 1% for international students. For the time being, all undergraduate courses in UKM use Bahasa Melayu as the medium of instruction.

Give2UKM
PhD Programme
Overview
Entry Requirements
Language Requirement
Programme Structure
Duration of Study
Fees
Form
The goal of the PhD programme is to prepare the next generation of leading researchers. The PhD is a programme of an independent, self-directed academic research degree, supported by a team of supervisors that makes an original contribution to knowledge written up in a publishable thesis and high impact journals. The PhD programme also supports the development of research and generic skills to equip students to operate successfully as a professional researcher in any setting.
Applicants applying for the Doctoral should have the following qualification:
A Master Degree in Information Technology/Computer Science from Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia or other universities approved by the Senate with a good Cummulative Grade Point Average (CGPA);
Other relevant qualifications or experience approved by the Senate
Currently enrolled in Master by research in FTSM and supported by Faculty’s Postgraduate Committee to convert to Doctor of Philosophy programme.
English Language Requirements
International students are required to fulfill the English language requirement as follows:
Score not less than 550 in TOEFL; or
not less than 5.5 in IELTS for particular programme.
International students who have the academic qualifications obtained from any local university approved by the Senate will be exempted from requirement (1)(a) but are required to take Ujian Kecekapan Bahasa Inggeris (UKBI) and if they do not acquire the level set by the program, the candidates are required to take and pass Modul Kemahiran Bahasa Inggeris (MKBI).
Faculty/Institute may grant exceptions to the English requirement for international students from countries with English as the official language or have academic qualifications obtained from institutions that use English as a medium of instruction.
Malay Language Requirements
Malay Language Courses (4 units)
All international students are required to follow two (2) Malay language courses equivalent to 4 units offered by the Citra Centre. Exemptions can be given to candidates who have taken and passed Malay language courses organized by other bodies recognized by the UKM Senate.
Doctor of Philosophy programme requires a candidate to do a research in the selected field and take the Research Methodology course. At the end of the programme, the candidate is required to prepare a thesis. The thesis has to be presented and defended in an oral examination which will be examined by examiners appointed by the faculty. Throughout the course of study, candidates are required to adhere to the following requirements :
Faculty Courses (4 Units)
Student must take and pass the Research Methodology course.
Proposal Defense and Research Progress
Student is required to submit research proposal and progress to the committee members between 6 months to 2 years of their study in order to continue their study at PhD level. Failure to submit their research proposal and progress may cause their studies to be terminated.
Publication Requirement
Students are required to produce at least TWO publications with a minimum of 2 journal articles (ISI/Scopus).
However, the PhD student can write to the Dean of FTSM/Director of UKM Postgraduate Office to apply for exemption from the minimum duration of study requirement, subject to approval from the Senate, having met the following conditions:
Has received approval from the Faculty/Centre for Academic Management
Has published at least two articles in high impact journals in the relevant research field, or in other journals endorsed by the Senate
The PhD student who is exempted under subregulation 5, has to pay the tuition fees for the duration of minimum study requirement.
Full time (6 – 12 semesters)
Part time (8 – 14 semesters)

We facilatate talent to innovate and create sustainable solutions, transforming ideas into thriving startups. By providing access to talent, markets, and investment, we help businesses scale and achieve lasting success
INNOVATE   •   INSPIRE   •   TRANSFORM
Mission, Vision & Objective
Mission
To mobilise entrepreneurial activities based on innovative and unique information technology in a conducive atmosphere
Vision
The establishment of unique information technology entrepreneurs based on innovative research
Objective
Memacu keusahawanan pelajar FTSM bersama CESMED dan CoE Smart Selangor
Memantau dan membimbing pelajar dalam aktiviti keusahawanan
Menyedia platform keusahawanan kondusif
Membimbing usahawan pelajar kepada dunia perniagaan sebenar bersama Mentor Industri melalui spin-off, spin-out dan start-up companies
Memprogramkan minda dan budaya keusahawanan di kalangan pelajar
Our Team
Dr. Saidah Saad
Assistant Dean (Entrepreneurship)
Ts. Dr. Ibrahim Mohamed
Ts. Dr. Fadilla 'Atyka Nor Rashid
Ts. Dr. Nazhatul Hafizah Kamarudin
Ts. Mohd Zamri Murah
History
InnoFarm began with small entrepreneurial ideas focusing on the need to commercialize research products. Initially, it was known as the "Incubator," but this name was rejected for being less appealing and not widely accepted. After some discussion, the name "Innovation Park" or "Innovation Farm" was chosen and eventually shortened to InnoFarm.
Located in Block D, InnoFarm is a key initiative in developing a conducive ecosystem for innovation and commercialization. This block is known as the "Innovation Curve," which includes several key components such as the Innovation Space on the ground floor, the Innovation Studio on the third floor, and InnoFarm on the fourth floor.
Since its establishment, InnoFarm has become a hub for various student entrepreneurial activities. These include courses such as LMCW1022, CMIE2013 Basic Entrepreneurship I, CMIE2023 Basic Entrepreneurship II, and CMIE3016 Advanced Entrepreneurship, as well as various commercialization initiatives.
In the early stages of its establishment, InnoFarm received visits from several external organizations, including :
Lembaga Pengarah Universiti (LPU)
Pusat Inovasi dan Kreativiti (PIK)
Universitas Islam Negeri Maulana Malik Ibrahim, Indonesia
Huawei
Technology Park Malaysia
MOHE Entrepreneurial Awards (MEA)

Karnival Latihan Industri & Kerjaya (KILK)
The Industrial Training Program is compulsory for students at the Faculty of Information Science and Technology. The program aims to provide students with exposure to a real working environment and practical experience to enhance their skills and develop the competencies required to become professionals, entrepreneurs, or researchers in the field of information technology.

Give2UKM
Final Year Project
The course trains students to conduct a project individually. Through this project, students are able to integrate knowledge and skills acquired during the study for solving a specific problem. This course requires students to prepare project documentation such as proposal/project plan, requirements specification, design specification and test specification. Based on the documents, students need to develop a computing product by using the appropriate methods, techniques and tools within a stipulated time frame. The students later have to present the documents and product as project deliverables to the designated stakeholders. The deliverables shall demonstrate sufficient level of complexity with respect to the chosen computing field.
Objective
Through the project, students aim to achieve the following objectives:
To integrate existing and new knowledge related to computing fields.
To develop a computing product based on the proposed methods and techniques.
To present the developed computing product verbally and in writing.
To produce a computing product that possesses entrepreneurial values.
To practise responsible, ethical and professional attitudes.
Sistem Pengurusan Inovasi Digital (SPID)
Karnival Inovasi Digital (KID)
KID Result
Karnival Inovasi Digital (KID) is an annual event showcasing the final year project outcomes of students from the Faculty of Technology and Information Science. This event serves as a platform for students to exhibit their achievements and discoveries in technology and digital innovation. Additionally, it provides an opportunity for students to share ideas and knowledge gained throughout their final year project journey with lecturers and peers.
A total of 15 Best Project Awards, known as the Digital Innovation Awards, and five (5) Entrepreneurship Awards are presented to honor students’ achievements. Winners are selected based on respective evaluation rubrics by the academic program committee and thee entrepreneurship and creative committee.

Give2UKM
Student Mobility & Exchange Program
Student mobility is one of UKM's and FTSM’s strategic priorities. Student mobility or exchange programs offer students the chance to study or conduct research at partner institutions abroad, enhancing students' academic and cultural experiences. These programs enable students to spend a semester or academic year at a partner university, where they can take courses that count towards their degree at UKM or work on research projects. Mobility and exchange programs are coordinated by the Office of International Relations Center with collaboration from various UKM faculties and institutes. For general information on the mobility programs at UKM, please visit:
www.ukm.my/pha
Embarking on a mobility program is a gateway to an extraordinary adventure that can redefine your academic and personal journey. Immerse yourself in a new culture, gain fresh perspectives from diverse teaching methods, and access unique courses that broaden your academic experience in ways your home institution may not offer. This experience goes beyond traditional learning; it cultivates essential life skills such as adaptability, independence, and cross-cultural communication. Engaging with a global network of peers and professionals enhances your resume and deepens your understanding of the world, making you more prepared to thrive in a dynamic, interconnected global landscape. Embrace the opportunity to transform your education and expand your world your future self will thank you for it!
Inbound Student
Outbound Student

We are excited to welcome inbound students worldwide to join our vibrant academic and research communities. We are committed to supporting your academic and personal growth during your time with us. Research Internship Program
This program enriches your academic experience through mentorship from experienced researchers and professionals, offers hands-on research projects at our institution, and provides a unique opportunity to apply theoretical knowledge to real-world challenges. As a participant, you'll gain international exposure to diverse research environments, enhancing your global perspective and intercultural competence. The duration of the research internship may vary depending on the specific project and arrangement with the host institution.
Students interested in this program should typically be enrolled in a relevant academic program, demonstrate a strong academic record, and have a keen interest in pursuing research. Potential mentors will interview students as part of the selection process.
Summer School@FTSM
Will be updated
All local and international students are welcome to apply for the student exchange program. Before you start, please read and understand the general Inbound Students Information related to requirements, procedures, and funds provided by the UKM International Relations Centre at
www.ukm.my/pha
. For the latest information on mobility opportunities, follow them on Facebook at
www.facebook.com/OIRUKM
Semester exchange program
This program offers students a chance to study at a partner institution abroad for one academic semester, allowing you to immerse yourself in a new educational and cultural environment while earning credits toward your degree at our institution. This program provides academic opportunities to enroll in courses that align with your goals, transfer credits back to your program, and have a unique cultural experience, enabling you to engage with local students and gain a broader global perspective. Additionally, you'll be able to build valuable connections with international peers and professionals, enhancing your global network.
Research Internship / Visiting Students Program
This program offers students the chance to undertake research internships or visit partner institutions abroad, providing hands-on experience in their field of study. This program enables you to work closely with researchers or academic professionals on significant projects, enhancing your academic and professional growth. To be eligible, you must have a strong academic record and demonstrate a clear research interest relevant to the program.
FTSM students interested in applying to the faculty's mobility fund must submit a formal request to the Mobility Coordinator. Application procedures are as follows:
Submit your request to the Mobility Secretariat via email at
mobiliti.ftsm@ukm.edu.my
For credit-transfer programs, students must identify course information for course equivalency :
1. Identify the courses you wish to take and obtain course information for equivalency.
2. Identify courses that match at UKM.
3. Your head of program will review applications and provide feedback on course equivalency.
4. Attend a faculty-level interview with the mobility coordinator and head of the program.
5. Successful candidates will receive an email notification with a pledge form from the faculty.
6. Complete and submit the pledge form.
7. Finalize university-level application documentation.
8. Receive a confirmation letter.
9. Submit a program report to mobiliti.ftsm@ukm.edu.my within two weeks of returning.
10.Apply for a credit transfer within two weeks of returning.
The JAIST Partner University Master Connection Program (JUMP) is an academic initiative between the Japan Advanced Institute of Science and Technology (JAIST) and Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia (UKM). The official signing ceremony for the JUMP program's Memorandum of Agreement (MoA) took place on November 4, 2024, at the UKM Chancellery.
JUMP serves as a pathway for UKM students to engage in research internships and pursue a master’s program at JAIST. UKM undergraduate students are eligible to apply. Selected candidates will complete an internship lasting a minimum of one week and a maximum of three months, followed by enrollment in the master's program (October intake) at JAIST, with financial support provided by JAIST.
Jump Program
www.ftsm.ukm.my/jump

Industry and Community
Industry
Community
Industrial Training
Reskill and upskill Program
At FTSM, our mission through HEJIM is to foster strategic, sustainable, and impactful engagements with industry partners on national, regional, and international levels. We are dedicated to establishing and nurturing strategic partnerships with a diverse array of stakeholders, from renowned multinational corporations to local companies. These partnerships are designed to promote the faculty by sharing reciprocal benefits, thereby strengthening and supporting both the university and the faculty in numerous ways.
Our collaborations extend beyond simple cooperation, encompassing a wide range of activities, including education, research, training, and services. By working closely with industry leaders such as Jabil, Keysight, Lenovo, Schlumberger, and many more. We ensure that our academic endeavors align with real-world needs and challenges. This synergy enhances the quality of our educational programs and drives innovative research.
Through targeted training programs, we equip our students and faculty with advanced skills and knowledge, preparing them to thrive in their respective fields. These programs are often tailored to the specific needs of our partners, ranging from advanced technological solutions to specialized industrial training.
Our services, developed in collaboration with our partners, offer practical solutions and advancements that benefit the industry. By addressing local and global challenges, we aim to make a tangible difference in the sector, from streamlining supply chains to fostering innovation in product development.
Together, we drive innovation, advance knowledge, and create lasting positive impacts in the industry. Our commitment to excellence and collaboration ensures that FTSM remains at the forefront of industry developments, fostering a dynamic environment that benefits both our students and our partners.
FTSM, through HEJIM, also places a strong emphasis on community engagement. Our partnerships with local community groups, particularly in rural areas including school, are aimed at promoting the faculty and fostering reciprocal benefits that strengthen both the university and the broader community.
Our collaborations in the community encompass a wide range of activities, including education, research, training, and services. By working closely with community leaders and organizations, we ensure that our academic endeavors address real-world needs and challenges, especially in underrepresented and rural areas.
Through targeted training programs, we equip our students and faculty with advanced skills and knowledge, preparing them to make meaningful contributions in their respective fields. These programs are often tailored to the specific needs of our community partners, ranging from grassroots community development initiatives to environmental sustainability projects. For example, our collaboration in rural areas has led to the development of accessible IT solutions, benefiting the local economy and improving technological inclusion.
Our services, developed in collaboration with our partners, offer practical solutions and advancements that benefit the broader community. By fostering these connections, HEJIM at FTSM continues to be a leader in community engagement, driving positive change and creating opportunities for growth and development. Our efforts contribute to building a resilient and inclusive society where everyone has the chance to thrive.
Our community engagement activities :
AWS Cloud Foundation Programme

Computer Lab & Internet
Monday - Friday
8:30 AM - 4:30 PM
Block C, G & H
The faculty is equipped with computer labs for both undergraduate and postgraduate students. For students who have their own laptops, connections to the Internet is available via the open Wifi hotpots around the faculty.
List of Computer Lab
Research Laboratory
Monday - Friday
8:30 AM - 4:30 PM
Block C, G & H
The faculty has several research laboratories are workplaces for the conduct of scientific research especially for Master and PhD program
List of Research Lab
Lecture Halls
Monday - Friday
8:30 AM - 4:30 PM
Block C & G
The faculty lecture halls have been designed to conduct lectures for 100 students to conferences to over 200 participants in a single seating.
Dewan Multimedia
Anjung Digital Smart Selangor UKM
Lecture Rooms
Monday - Friday
8:30 AM - 4:30 PM
Block B & G
Here at FTSM, students are provided with classrooms that can fit 60/70 students at a time. All classrooms are equipped with audio-visual, media and computing facilities to support learning in class.
List of Lecture Rooms

Monday - Friday
8:30 AM - 4:30 PM
Level 2, Block D
8 seat
Raazzuriyati Razali
Faculty has one Multimedia Studio offers FTSM students to make a multimedia activity.
FTSM Library
Monday - Friday
8:30 AM - 4:30 PM
Level 2, Block H
Saiful Azha Ahmad
The Faculty has a library which provides a conducive environment where student can browse through books, magazines, periodical journals and theses of graduate students. In addition to these, the library also provides access to the Journal of Information Technology and Multimedia, a journal by the Faculty.
Meeting & Viva Room
Monday - Friday
8:30 AM - 4:30 PM
Block A, B & G
Ideal for meetings and discussions, each meeting room features fixed conference arrangement seating with adjustable chairs.
List of Meeting Room
Viva Room
Executive Discussion Room
Monday - Friday
8:30 AM - 4:30 PM
Level 2, Block G
48 seat
Muhedir Mohamad
Ideal for meetings, discussions and seminar, each meeting room features fixed conference arrangement seating with adjustable chairs. The meeting rooms contain ceiling mounted projectors, and a drop-down screen.
Innovation Space
Monday - Friday
8:30 AM - 4:30 PM
Level 1, Block D
30 seat
The Innovation Space is a vast area furnished with seating and studying spaces. It is where students congregate, relax, plan, study and do all the things that make student life memorable. It is truly a relaxing environment where students can engage with their peers, have fun, and unwind.
Cafeteria
Monday - Friday
8:30 AM - 4:30 PM
Ground Floor, Block G
Our cafeteria serves a wide range of halal and Local food. It is a popular hang-out spot for students during breaks, as they munch on some snacks and delectable treats while having a group discussion or while working on their assignments.
Prayer Room
Monday - Friday
8:30 AM - 4:30 PM
Block E
The male and female suraus located on the first & second floor, Block E are available for Muslim students and staff when they find it difficult to attend the mosque prayers in between classes.

Bicara Alumni FTSM Tersohor pada 30 September 2024. Dr. Haji Amirudin Abdul Wahab, CEO Cybersecurity Malaysia (Alumni 1999), dan Prof. Dr. Zarina Binti Shukur, Profesor Pusat Cyber Security, FTSM

Fakulti Teknologi dan Sains Maklumat menjemput anda semua untuk…
Dewan Tun Abdullah Mohd Salleh (DTAMS), UKM, Bangi
13 Apr 2026
Read More »
Karnival Latihan Industri dan Kerjaya (KLIK) 2026
Jom ke Karnival Latihan Industri dan Kerjaya (KLIK) 2026!
Fakulti Teknologi dan Sains Maklumat menjemput anda semua untuk hadir ke program yang penuh dengan peluang, perkongsian dan pendedahan industri!
Menarik untuk anda:
• Sesi perkongsian eksklusif bersama industri seperti - Knowledgecom Corporation, Lenovo Malaysia, U Mobile & Ebright Sdn.Bhd.
• Sesi perkongsian peluang melanjutkan pengajian ke peringkat siswazah
• Reruai Pemeran oleh Knowledgecom, Lenovo, U Mobile, Ebright, DeltaSpike, UKM Karier & Unit Siswazah FTSM
• Penilaian Pembentangan Latihan Industri
Karnival ini bertujuan memperkasa pelajar melalui pembentangan dan penilaian projek yang dilaksana sepanjang penempatan pelajar di industri.
Semua warga FTSM dijemput hadir.
**Pengangkutan bas ulang alik percuma disediakan FTSM ke DTAMS. Sila rujuk poster untuk jadual coaster.
**Makanan dan minuman disediakan untuk semua pelajar.
FTSM Peneraju Teknologi Digital • Ilmu, Mutu dan Budi

RICH SOFTAM 2026
TEKAD 57 TAHUN UKM
RICH SOFTAM 2026–2030
Pusat Kajian SOFTAM
FTSM, UKM
Konsep Acara
Apa itu
RICH
Program tahunan perdana Pusat Kajian SOFTAM yang menyatukan akademia, industri dan komuniti dalam satu ekosistem inovasi yang holistik.
Research
Pembentangan hasil penyelidikan terkini menerusi SOFTECH 2026, penilaian poster, dan sesi pertandingan 3/5MT oleh pelajar siswazah SOFTAM.
Industry
Jaringan strategik dengan industri melalui ucaptama TalentCorp, forum dengan Kumpulan Wang Simpanan Pekerja (KWSP), Malaysian Industry-Government Group for High Technology (MIGHT) & Tenaga Nasional Berhad (TNB), pameran industri, dan pertandingan inovasi.
Community
Pemindahan ilmu kepada komuniti melalui bengkel umum AR/VR & kepimpinan, pameran inovasi, & program outreach SOFTAM.
outreacH
Memperluas jangkauan SOFTAM kepada alumni, komuniti luar dan pelajar & membina ekosistem penyelidikan inklusif serta berdaya saing.
Pengisian Program
Aktiviti
Utama
Komponen utama RICH 2026 yang merangkumi akademia, industri dan komuniti.
01 — Pelajar
SOFTAM Postgradute Seminar
Pembentangan Lisan PhD, Pameran Poster Penyelidikan, dan Pertandingan 3/5MT oleh pelajar siswazah SOFTAM.
02 — Pensyarah
SOFTECH 2026
Pembentangan manuskrip mengenai inovasi perisian dan pengurusan teknologi.
03 — Terbuka
Pertandingan Inovasi RICH
Terbuka kepada Universiti Awam dan Swasta, Kolej, Politeknik dan industri. 4 kategori inovasi, sasaran 40 peserta. Hadiah Emas, Perak, Gangsa + Anugerah Terbaik.
04 — Bengkel
Bengkel Profesional
Bengkel Kepimpinan
VOICE Leading with Impact: Mastering Strategic Communication
(agensi/industri) dan Bengkel Teknologi AR/VR Revolusi PdP: Cipta Pengalaman Imersif AR & VR Tanpa Stress (guru, pensyarah, pendidik).
05 — Ucaptama
Ucaptama & Forum Industri
Ucaptama akademik (Prof. Emeritus Dr. Abdul Razak Hamdan) dan industri (TalentCorp). Forum panel bersama Kumpulan Wang Simpanan Pekerja (KWSP),
Malaysian Industry-Government Group for High Technology
(MIGHT) dan Tenaga Nasional Berhad (TNB).
06 — Pameran
Pameran & Promosi SOFTAM
Pameran industri, promosi HEJIM & program siswazah, pameran poster penyelidikan dan pelancaran Buku Strategi SOFTAM.
Penceramah & Panel
Pakar &
Pemimpin Industri
Barisan penceramah jemputan daripada akademia, industri dan agensi kerajaan terkemuka Malaysia.
Prof. Emeritus Dr. Abdul Razak Hamdan
Profesor Emeritus FTSM, UKM
Empowering Future Talent in software technology and management.
— Ucaptama Akademik
Dr. Devaki Nagaya
Vice President of Sector Development at MyMahir, TalentCorp
Preparing Future Researcher for AI, Digital and Green Economy
— Ucaptama Industri
Prof. Dr. Nurhizam Safie Mohd Satar
SOFTAM, FTSM UKM
Moderator Forum Industri:
What's Next for Researchers?

SOFTECH 2026
SOFTAM Mini Conference of Software Innovation Technology Management
Tentang SOFTECH2026
SOFTECH 2026 merupakan sebahagian daripada pengisian utama program RICH SOFTAM 2026.
Ia dianjurkan sebagai platform akademik bagi membentangkan hasil penyelidikan, idea kajian semasa, projek pelajar serta inisiatif secara tersusun.
SOFTECH 2026 berperanan sebagai pemangkin dalam memupuk budaya penyelidikan dan perkongsian ilmu yang lebih dinamik, khususnya dalam kalangan ahli SOFTAM.
Tema
Empowering Digital Transformation in Software Technology and Management
Trek
Teknologi Perisian
Pengurusan Teknologi

Pertandingan
Pertandingan
Inovasi RICH
Pertandingan Inovasi RICH bertujuan untuk mencungkil inovasi dan kreativiti peserta melalui pembentangan idea serta pembangunan prototaip.
Pertandingan ini terbuka kepada sektor industri dan warga Institusi Pendidikan Tinggi bagi mewujudkan persekitaran kolaboratif untuk mempamerkan hasil kerja
masing-masing. Dengan memberi tumpuan kepada penyelesaian berimpak tinggi dalam pelbagai sektor, pertandingan ini mengetengahkan peranan penting
pemikiran inovatif dalam memacu kemajuan dalam bidang teknologi, pendidikan, dan pembangunan sosial.
Objektif Pertandingan
Pertandingan Inovasi RICH bertujuan untuk:
● Menjadi platform untuk mengenal pasti serta menyerlahkan potensi kreatif peserta melalui pembentangan idea-idea baharu.
● Menggalakkan penghasilan penyelesaian baharu yang mempunyai nilai komersial, potensi Harta Intelek (IP),
serta impak nyata dalam bidang pengajaran dan pembelajaran (PdP), penyelidikan, industri, dan komuniti.
Syarat Penyertaan
Peserta perlu memenuhi syarat-syarat berikut:
● Terbuka kepada peserta dari Institusi Pendidikan Tinggi (Universiti Awam, Universiti Swasta, Kolej, Politeknik, Institut) dan pihak industri.
● Peserta boleh menyertai sebagai:
○ Individu, atau
○ Pasukan (maksimum 2–5 ahli)
● Setiap peserta boleh menghantar lebih daripada satu penyertaan bagi setiap kategori.
Kategori Pertandingan
Peserta boleh memilih untuk menyertai mana-mana kategori berikut:
Inovasi Pengajaran dan Pembelajaran (PdP)
Pendekatan inovatif bagi meningkatkan nilai dan kualiti PdP dengan penggunaan teknologi digital.
Inovasi Penyelidikan
Cetusan idea baharu dalam teknologi pengkomputeran dan perisian seperti kejuruteraan perisian, interaksi manusia-komputer,
kecerdasan buatan, pembelajaran mesin, sains data, keselamatan siber dan sebagainya.
Inovasi Industri & Komersial
Menyerlahkan projek dengan kerelevanan industri dan potensi pengkomersialan yang kukuh seperti pembangunan prototaip,
produk atau penyelesaian yang dibangunkan.
Inovasi Komuniti & Sosial
Inisiatif baharu dalam menangani keperluan masyarakat dan mewujudkan impak sosial yang positif yang melibatkan komuniti.
Prosedur Pertandingan
1. Penghantaran Abstrak (Sebelum 12 Jun 2026)
Abstrak perlu memenuhi kriteria berikut:
Penulisan boleh dibuat dalam Bahasa Inggeris atau Bahasa Melayu.
Kandungan mestilah merangkumi antara 250 hingga 300 patah perkataan.
Nyatakan maklumat lengkap peserta serta ahli pasukan, termasuk nama dan institusi atau organisasi.
Melantik seorang ahli sebagai penulis koresponden dan menyediakan alamat emel yang aktif.
Penyediaan abstrak boleh merujuk kepada templat yang disediakan:
Templat
Penghantaran abstrak hendaklah dibuat melalui emel berikut:
softamrich.ftsm@ukm.edu.my
2. Keputusan rasmi saringan akan dimaklumkan kepada penulis koresponden melalui emel pada 15-19 Jun 2026.
Peserta yang berjaya perlu:
Melengkapkan borang pendaftaran melalui pautan dalam emel notifikasi
Menjelaskan bayaran yuran penyertaan sebanyak RM350.
3. Pada hari pertandingan, peserta diberi 10 minit untuk membentang termasuk sesi soal jawab.
Peserta perlu menyediakan bahan pembentangan dan prototaip (jika ada) mengikut garis panduan ini:
Pengenalan: Masalah yang ingin diselesaikan.
Penyelesaian/Inovasi: Penjelasan mengenai idea atau produk, termasuk cara prototaip dibangunkan atau kajian dijalankan.
Impak & Komersialisasi: Potensi kegunaan kepada masyarakat atau industri.
Demonstrasi Prototaip/Output Inovasi: Gambar, video pendek (maksimum 2 minit), pautan simulasi, atau prototaip sebenar.
4. Kaedah pembentangan adalah secara
Bring Your Own Device (BYOD).
Peserta boleh menggunakan peranti sendiri seperti komputer riba, tablet atau paparan skrin (contohnya TV/monitor) untuk tujuan pembentangan.
Peserta digalakkan menyediakan bahan sokongan seperti poster atau bunting mengikut kreativiti bagi meningkatkan keberkesanan penyampaian.

Bengkel 1 — Kepimpinan
VOICE Leading with Impact: Mastering Strategic Communication
Shaping Visionary Leaders for Tomorrow
5 Modul :
● Komunikasi Strategik
● Kejelasan Mesej
● Pengaruh Stakeholder
● Kehadiran Eksekutif
● Kepimpinan Perubahan
Sasaran Peserta
Agensi, Industri dan NGO – Pemimpin Kanan, Pengarah dan Pengurus
Yuran Pendaftaran
RM200 (Peserta Luar)
Instruktor
Prof. Madya Dr. Yazrina Yahya
Associate Professor Dr. Yazrina Yahya is the Director of Pusat PERMATA@Pintar Negara, UKM, and a Certified HRD Corp Trainer. Formerly a leader at AKEPT and UKM Global, she specializes in strategic communication,
leadership sustainability, and internationalization. Her cross-sector expertise bridging academia, industry (AirAsia), and national policy empowers leaders to drive impactful global change.
Bengkel 2 — Teknologi AR/VR
Revolusi PdP: Cipta Pengalaman Imersif AR & VR Tanpa Stress
Teknologi Augmented Reality & Virtual Reality untuk Pendidikan
Apa yang peserta dapat:
● Memahami asas teknologi AR/VR dan potensinya dalam merevolusikan pengajaran dan pembelajaran (PdP).
● Membangunkan pengalaman dan prototaip AR/VR untuk PdP secara mudah dan praktikal.
● Meningkatkan keyakinan menghasilkan kandungan AR/VR secara santai dan tanpa stress.
Sasaran Peserta
Guru, Pensyarah, Tutor dam Pendidik Kretif.
Yuran Pendaftaran
RM200 (Peserta Luar)
Instruktor
Dr. Rimaniza Zainal Abidin
Dr. Rimaniza merupakan seorang pensyarah di Fakulti Teknologi dan Sains Maklumat (FTSM), Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia (UKM). Bidang kepakaran beliau merangkumi Realiti Lanjutan (XR), Interaksi Manusia-Komputer (HCI) dan Pengaturcaraan Mudah Alih.
Beliau terlibat secara aktif dalam penyelidikan berkaitan teknologi XR, terutamanya dalam pembangunan aplikasi interaktif dan teknik interaksi adaptif untuk meningkatkan pengalaman pengguna. Selain pengajaran dan pembelajaran, beliau juga terlibat
dalam pembangunan aplikasi mudah alih serta penyelidikan inovatif dalam teknologi digital merentasi pelbagai sektor, termasuk pelancongan dan pendidikan.

Prof. Dr. Abdul Razak Hamdan
KEYNOTE ACADEMIC:
Empowering Future Talent in Software Technology and Management
Prof. Dr. Abdul Razak Hamdan earned a BSc in Mathematics from Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia (1975), an MSc in Computing from Newcastle University (1977), and a PhD in Artificial Intelligence from Loughborough University (1987).
He is an Emeritus Professor at UKM’s Centre for Artificial Intelligence Technology, with research interests in AI, data mining, analytics, data science, decision support systems, IT strategy, and software quality.
Dr. Devaki Nagaya
KEYNOTE INDUSTRY:
Preparing Future Researcher for AI, Digital and Green Economy.
Dr. Devaki Nagaya is the Vice President of Sector Development at MyMahir, TalentCorp. Leveraging over 23 years of experience across academia,
research, and workforce development, she drives national talent strategies to future-proof Malaysia’s workforce. Dr. Devaki holds a PhD in Molecular Genetics,
an MSc in Pharmacogenetics (USM), and a BSc in Genetics (UKM). In her current role, she spearheads critical industry-academia collaborations to close talent gaps and align regional skillsets with TalentCorp’s national mandate.
Dr. Zamros Dzulkifli
KEYNOTE INDUSTRY: Panel 1
What's Next for Researchers?
Dr Zamros Dzulkafli is currently the Head of Macro Strategy & Economics Research at the Employees Provident Fund (EPF). He advises EPF Management on the global economy to ensure the best
investment strategies are adopted to maximise investment returns. He was a Senior Economist at Maybank Investment Bank (from 2103-2024), focusing on econometrics research. He has spearheaded strategic initiatives to enhance
fund sustainability and national economic resilience.
Dr. Tan Shu Ying
KEYNOTE INDUSTRY: Panel 2
What's Next for Researchers?
Dr. Tan is a Senior Principal Analyst at myForesight, Malaysian Industry-Government Group for High Technology (MIGHT), with expertise in foresight, strategic analysis, policymaking, and capacity
building. She has led and contributed to studies, trainings, and advisory bodies at the sectoral, national, and regional levels, helping organisations and
policymakers anticipate change and navigate the future.
Ir. Ts. Bibi Hazrina Alli Rahman
KEYNOTE INDUSTRY: Panel 3
What's Next for Researchers?
An engineering professional at Tenaga Nasional Berhad (TNB), Bibi Hazrina leads talent capability development at ILSAS. She specializes in energy transition, HSE and
technical regulatory competency, designing large-scale upskilling initiatives to advance workforce readiness and support the evolution of Malaysia’s energy
landscape.
Prof. Madya Dr. Yazrina Yahya
Instruktor Bengkel Kepimpinan
VOICE Leading with Impact: Mastering Strategic Communication
Associate Professor Dr. Yazrina Yahya is the Director of Pusat PERMATA@Pintar Negara, UKM, and a Certified HRD Corp Trainer. Formerly a leader at AKEPT and UKM Global, she specializes in strategic communication,
leadership sustainability, and internationalization. Her cross-sector expertise bridging academia, industry (AirAsia), and national policy empowers leaders to drive impactful global change.
Dr. Rimaniza Zainal Abidin
Instruktor BENGKEL AR/VR:
Revolusi PdP: Cipta Pengalaman Imersif AR & VR Tanpa Stress
Dr. Rimaniza merupakan seorang pensyarah di Fakulti Teknologi dan Sains Maklumat (FTSM), Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia (UKM). Bidang kepakaran beliau merangkumi Realiti Lanjutan (XR), Interaksi Manusia-Komputer (HCI) dan Pengaturcaraan Mudah Alih.
Beliau terlibat secara aktif dalam penyelidikan berkaitan teknologi XR, terutamanya dalam pembangunan aplikasi interaktif dan teknik interaksi adaptif yang bertujuan untuk meningkatkan pengalaman pengguna. Selain pengajaran dan pembelajaran, Dr. Rimaniza juga terlibat
dalam pembangunan aplikasi mudah alih serta menjalankan penyelidikan inovatif dalam teknologi digital merentasi pelbagai sektor, termasuk pelancongan dan pendidikan.

# Programme with exam and interview
International and Malaysian Students - UKM International Programme (UKMi)
If you are ready for a mix of quality education and cultural diversity, then you have come to the right place. Here in UKM, education is the priority of our highly qualified professionals who are not just here to share their knowledge but also to assist you in embracing the cultural variety that our campus has to offer.
Open for local students and international students*
With our UKM International programme, you will have the opportunity to pursue your degree and travel overseas for a summer programme. Throughout the years, UKM has formed strong partnerships with the higher learning institutions in countries such as Korea, Japan, Indonesia, Vietnam, China, Mexico, Germany and the United States. You will have the chance to travel to any of the mentioned countries for a one-month summer programme, which will allow you to participate in activities such as learning the local languages, sightseeing and mixing with the locals. So what are you waiting for? Come enrol with us now!
Apply Now!

NURHAYU BINTI KAMARUDIN

“I am very grateful and satisfied to follow the programme offered by UKM through PKP. Executive study mode on weekend lectures is very appropriate & flexible especially for me as a mother, employee and student at the same time. The Master of Science (Quality and Productivity Improvement) is a programme that is very much in line with my job scope and duties at the Ministry of Education Malaysia. Thank you UKM!”
NAZZATUL DASYEEMA BINTI BAHAR
Principal Assistant Director
Management Research Division, MAMPU
Graduated Year 2017

AHMAD ZULKARNAIN BIN AHMAD MARZUKI

Business administration , 2017
China
"When I came to UKM, I fell in love with this place.  Because there are so many green plants, I can breathe very fresh air.  I chose UKM to study because my previous university did not have a good reputation as UKM, and the QS ranking was not as high as UKM.  I wish I could get a better education, so I came.  It is worth mentioning that there are various restaurants in UKM for dining."
LIU SIQIAN
Bachelor Business Administration, 2017
China
"Comfortable and beautiful environment, strong teaching staff, Reasonable tuition"

OFFICIAL ANNOUNCEMENT: RESTRUCTURING OF BACHELOR OF DOCTOR IN MEDICINE
(MDUKM) FACULTY OF MEDICINE (FPER) FOR LOCAL AND INTERNATIONAL STUDENTS
To whom it may concern,
Please be informed that The National University of Malaysia (UKM), through its Centre for Shaping
Advanced & Professional Education (UKMShape), is currently undertaking a restructuring of the tuition
fees for the Bachelor of Medicine (MDUKM), Faculty of Medicine (FPER). The revised fee structure is
expected to be announced by 30 June 2026.
We regret for any inconvenience caused and thank you for your understanding.
Best regards
Director
Centre for Shaping Advanced and Professional Education
The National University of Malaysia

Finance Rules & Regulation for Student​
These general rules are issued as a guideline to students under the Shaping Advanced and Professional Education Center (UKMSHAPE), Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia (UKM):
International Undergraduate Students
UKMi International Mode
Open & Distance Learning (ODL) mode
International Postgraduate Students
Executive Mode
Full-time Mode
Open & Distance Learning (ODL) mode
This document shall be made as the main reference, with regards to student financial services in UKMSHAPE.
1.0 STUDY FEES
1.1  Study fees package that is charged during their entire course duration, comprise the items below:
Academic Fees;
Examination Fees;
Reading materials including study session modules- if applicable; and
Other fees such as examination fees, computer and science lab fees as well as students’ activities at faculties and universities or any other related fees, deemed applicable by the University
1.2 The amount of fees charged to each student may vary according to the faculty and level of study offered. Students may refer to the fee schedule which is attached to their offer letter. Further information related to study fees are also available in UKMSHAPE official website
www.ukm.my/ukmshape
. UKMSHAPE reserves the right to change the fee rate at any time without prior notice to students.
1.3 Types of fees other than tuition fees charged to students are as follows:
Registration fees
Registration Fee is a fee charged during student registration day which covers the entire student registration process. Registration fees amounting to up to RM3,000 (depending on the program) will not be refunded if the student withdraws from the offered study or rejects the offer beyond the set period.
Convocation Fee
The convocation fee is a fee charged to all students who successfully complete their studies at UKM. This fee is charged in the student’s final semester depending on the approval of the UKM Senate. This fee must be paid and does not depend on the student’s attendance at the Convocation Ceremony.
Short Semester Fee and Early Termination Fee
Executive Undergraduate Program: The short semester tuition fee will be charged to the student in the student’s final semester and increased as an early termination fee.
Programs other than Executive Undergraduate: The short semester fee has been included in the tuition fee.
1.4 For any additional semester below the maximum semester, students will be charged up to RM2500 per semester. Meanwhile, any additional semesters beyond the student’s maximum semester will be charged based on the full tuition fee per semester. (Refer to Table 4)
2.0 PAYMENT OPTIONS
A. UKMi International Undergraduate
2.1  Students may opt to pay their study fees by semesters. This means the total payment of study fee shall be distributed by the number of semesters, as indicated in the offer letter and payable in instalments.
2.2  The instalments mentioned above are outlined in the Table 1. Students are encouraged to contact UKMSHAPE Finance Division for further advise on each payment option offered.
OPTION 1
Students shall pay their semester study in full, before enrolling in each semester.
OPTION 2
Students shall arrange for semester full payment according to the timeline below:
40% of the semester study fee
BEFORE
student’s enrolment in each semester not later than two (2) weeks before the date of registration;
30% of the study fee
AFTER
student’s enrolment in the following month of each semester; and
The remaining 30% of the semester study fee after the second payment in the following month.
2.3  The following conditions are applicable to those who have selected
OPTION 2
Payment of study fees per semester shall be made within the stipulated timeline in para 2.2 (full payment must be received by the second month of each semester; and
In the event that payment is not received, the implications are as below:
i. will be
DISQUALIFIED
to register for the semester course;
ii. will be
NOT ELIGIBLE
to receive reading materials/study modules; and
iii. will be
BARRED
from obtaining final exam results of the semester.
2.4 The study fee charged to all students includes a Commitment Fee of RM3,000. This fee will not be refunded if the student withdraws from their programme.
Table 1
: Choice of method of payment of study fees according to the semester of study
OPTIONS
ILLUSTRATION
BEFORE REGISTRATION
AFTER REGISTRATION
Semester 1 and 2 intake
100% of the semester fee
Fee has been paid
Semester 1 intake (Sept/Oct)
40% of Semester 1 fees in September
30% in October and 30% in the following November in the same calendar year
Semester 2 intake (Feb/Mar)
40% of Semester 2 fees in February
30% in March and 30% in the following April in the same calendar year
B. International Postgraduate Executive, Full -Time Mode and Open & Distance Learning Mode (ODL).
2.5  The total study fee has been divided into payments according to the semester of study.
2.6  Students shall pay their semester study in full, before enrolling in each semester.
3.0 FINES AND PENALTIES
3.1  Fee refunds are subject to any outstanding balance/arrears in the student account. Depending on eligibility, the approval for a refund shall be made upon recalculation and confirming the final amount. UKMSHAPE reserves the right to withhold the study fees paid if the student withdraws or postpones / is suspended before the end of the study period.
3.2  Refund of fees is subject to the outstanding balance arrears by the student with recalculation and confirmed by the Finance Division of PKP.
3.3  Reimbursement of study fee to students who have withdrawn, suspended or before the end of the study period is subject to the rate determined by the UKMSHAPE as in
Table 2
below
No.
Details
Refundable Rate (%)
Students withdraw/postpone/dismissed before the lecture week begins
100%
of the current semester’s tuition fee
Students withdraw/suspend/ dismissed from studies in the first week (1) to the second week (2) of the semester
70%
of the current semester’s tuition fee
Students withdraw/postpone/ dismissed from studies in the third (3) to the sixth (6) week of the semester
50%
of the current semester’s tuition fee
Students withdraw/defer/ dismissed from studies after the sixth (6) week of the semester
study fee refund for the semester will be refunded.
4.0 READING MATERIALS AND STUDY MODULES (for selected programmes only)
4.1  Reading materials including study modules will be provided at the UKMSHAPE Book Counter as soon as the study session begins for each semester.
4.2  Reading materials including modules during the current session are subject to availability. Eligible students may claim their reading materials/ study modules during the relevant semester only and it cannot be claimed after the semester is complete. Students are only allowed to study reading material including study session modules within one semester only. UKMSHAPE reserves the right to use the existing stock to replace reading materials including study modules supplied to students.
4.3  Apart from the above, it is also reminded conditions set in para 2.3 (b) (ii) are also applicable before claiming the reading materials/study modules.
4.4  Reading materials including study session modules provided by UKMSHAPE are selected programmes onlyherwise. (No modules are provided for postgraduate and undergraduate students from Faculty of FSSK & FTSM).
4.5  Self-learning materials (ODL mode only) are provided through an online learning platform which is the Learning Management System (LMS).
5.0 MISCELLANEOUS PAYMENTS AND CHARGES
5.1 Students are subject to various fees and other charges set by the UKMSHAPE during the period of study as shown in
Table 3
5.2 The various fees set by the UKMSHAPE are divided based on the current needs of students as follows:
Study fees;
Miscellaneous fees; and
Fees – other fees

6.0 METHOD OF FEE PAYMENT
6.1  For students who pay study fees privately
The information is currently being updated. We apologize for any inconvenience this may have caused.
6.2  Study fees through sponsorship and study loans.
NO.
METHOD
DETAILS
Submission of Scholarship/Loan Offer Letter
Students who receive scholarships or loans shall submit to the UKMSHAPE (scholarship/loan agreement letter) to facilitate the handling of study fee claims by the UKMShape to the sponsor or lender.
**Note:
Any delay in submitting the offer letter to UKMShape will result in a delay in making a claim on the sponsor’s.
A student’s activities will be blocked if the sponsors are unable to settle the claim within the timeframe.
7.0 REVIEW OF STUDENT ACCOUNTS AND CORRESPONDENCE
7.1  UKMShape will issue official receipts for payments made by students. Students are required to keep the original receipt of study fees for their own record and reference purposes.
7.2 Students are able to check the status of study fee payments or various payments that have been made through the official e-finance portal of UKM at the address
https://ekewangan.ukm.my
7.3  Any correspondence and inquiries from students can be submitted to:
Finance Division

| APEL.Q Frequently Asked Questions (FAQ)
Fee Payment
Questions
Answers
1. What is the minimum fee I need to pay when completing self-registration in the e-Speed system? (applicable for new students only)
Students are required to pay at least 40% of the first semester’s fees.
For more information, please refer to the Finance Rules & Regulations:
https://www.ukm.my/ukmshape/finance-ukmshape/
2. Is the fee payment based on semesters or on assessment components?
Tuition fees are charged each semester, regardless of the number of components registered for that semester.
For more information regarding the tuition fee structure, please refer to the following link:
https://www.ukm.my/ukmshape/ukmshape-learning-fees/
3. When do I need to pay my tuition fees?
Students are required to pay their fees at the beginning of each new semester according to the amount specified in the tuition fee structure.
For more information, please refer to the Finance Rules & Regulations:
https://www.ukm.my/ukmshape/finance-ukmshape/
4. Where can I check the current fee amount/outstanding balance?
Students can check the current fee amount/outstanding balance in the e-Kewangan system:
https://ekewangan.ukm.my/
5. If I have been in an additional semester/exceeded the semesters outlined in the tuition fee structure, will I be charged an additional fee?
Yes. This is because the tuition fees are charged as a package and are based on the minimum study duration.
Therefore, students will be charged additional fees for the extended period and services provided.
6. If I fail a particular assessment component, will I be charged if I want to retake the assessment?
Yes. A fee of RM1000 will be charged to students who retake the APEL.Q assessment component for one retake (a maximum of 3 retakes, with a fee applied for each retake).
7. For the first fee payment, can I immediately apply for a KWSP withdrawal for the purpose of financing my tuition fees?
No, you cannot. Students must first be in a ‘Registered’ status before making any KWSP claims.
To achieve the ‘Registered’ status, students must complete all registration requirements in the e-Speed system, including the minimum payment mentioned in item (1).
Please check the ‘Registered’ status in the e-Speed system:
https://smp.ukm.my/espeed/main/
Procedures for Tuition Fee Payment via KWSP:
https://drive.google.com/file/d/1sLncEd_KIxILUZYk2izgP6Nx2U7OuHJ1/view?usp=sharing
Self-Registration (for new students)
Questions
Answers
1. If I intend to register in the e-Speed system in the middle of the semester or outside the new student registration period, is this allowed?
Yes. APEL.Q candidates are allowed to register at any time.
However, students are advised to check if there are any implications regarding tuition fees and the assessment process by the faculty.
2. For the ‘Medical Report’ registration requirement, can I have a medical check-up at any clinic/hospital/health center?
Yes, you can. However, students must bring the Report of Health Examination which is available in the e-Speed system, when undergoing the medical check-up.
This form can be downloaded through the e-Speed system at the following link:
https://smp.ukm.my/espeed/main/
3. For the ‘MySiswa Debit Card-i’ requirement, is it mandatory to open an I-Pro RHB savings account (RHB Pro Savings Account-I)?
Yes, students are required to open an RHB account in their own name after accepting the offer. Please note that opening this RHB Savings Account is mandatory to obtain an RHB MySiswa Debit Card and complete the self-registration process.
Registration for Assessment Components
Questions
Answers
1. Where can I register for the assessment
components each semester?
Students can register for the assessment components on the SMPE website:
https://smplucee.ukm.my/smpe
2. If I have not passed a particular assessment in the current semester, do I need to register for the same assessment component in the next semester?
Yes. If a student has not passed a particular assessment component in the current semester, the student must register for the same assessment component in the following semester.
3. Am I allowed to register for all components at the same time?
No. Students must register for the assessment components in sequence and are only allowed to register for the next component after passing the previous one.
Assessment Component Results
Questions
Answers
1. Where can I check the results of the assessment components?
Students can check the results of the assessment components on the SMPE website:
https://smplucee.ukm.my/smpe
2. When can I check my grades and Cumulative Grade Point Average (CGPA)?
Students can check their grades and Cumulative Grade Point Average (CGPA) at the end of each semester on the SMPE website:
https://smplucee.ukm.my/smpe
Withdraw / Defer Procedure
Questions
Answers
1. If I intend to withdraw or defer my studies, what is the process?
-For withdrawal applications, students can submit their requests through the SMPE website:
https://smplucee.ukm.my./smpe
-For deferment applications, students can download the form at the following link:
https://www.ukm.my/ukmshape/student-form/.
Students are advised to submit withdrawal or deferment applications before the start of a new semester to avoid any financial implications.
-For more information, please refer to the Finance Rules & Regulations:
https://www.ukm.my/ukmshape/finance-ukmshape/
UKMShape Address
Centre for Shaping Advanced & Professional Education (UKMSHAPE)

Attention to ALL ONE-OFF STUDENT PASS HOLDER, as part of our initiative to support students in maintaining valid documentation, we are offering a special opportunity to renew your i-Kad and insurance with reduced penalty fees
📄 **Please refer to the attached document for more information:**
👉 Refer to IKRE Newsletter PDF
A student pass allows multiple entry
Issued annually or one-off basis.
IMPORTANT REMINDER:
TIME TO RENEW!
ONE-OFF PASS RENEWAL REQUIREMENT
SUBMIT YOUR STUDENT PASS / IKAD & INSURANCE RENEWAL HERE
CHECK YOUR APPLICATION STATUS HERE
SUBMIT YOUR ADDITIONAL DOCUMENT HERE
FAQs
DOWNLOAD THE EXAMPLE DOCUMENT HERE
A  student pass renewal  application  is mandatory for any student whose  pass is valid for 1 year or less.
A student pass renewal should be applied as early as 3 months or at least 2 months before the expiry date.
Submission of renewal application is
mandatory, regardless of your current location
as it can be done online. If you are outside of
Malaysia, you are required to return to
Malaysia as soon as your application status
turns 50% or at least 2 weeks before your pass
(visa) expired.
Effective January 2025, EMGS will no longer accept bank  transfers for application fee payments. It is advisable to  have a Touch 'n Go eWallet account, as payments will  need to be made through JomPAY platform if you do not  have a local bank account.
WHAT IS ONE-OFF PASS?
A one-off pass is a student pass duration that is valid for more than 1 year
i-Kad Information
Insurance Package and Guidelines
It  is  compulsory  to renew your i-Kad and insurance
annually
, as early as 3 months or at least 2 months before the expiry month.
If your academic and attendance report does not satisfy the requirement of the Ministry of Higher Education, EMGS will not proceed with your application.
Late renewal penalty will be automatically applied if complete documents were received within 50 days before student pass expiry.
If no submission is received by 50 days before date of expiry. Visa Unit reserves the right to report to the Faculty and relevant authorities. The late renewal penalties will be charged to your tuition fee account.

[Page 63]
URL: https://www.ukm.my/portalukm/vision-mission
Title: Vision & Mission - Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia - The National University of Malaysia

Vision
UKM is committed to be ahead  of society and time in leading the development of  a learned, dynamic  and moral society
Motto
Iman Ilmu Amal
Philosophy
UKM affirms the integration of faith in Allah and constructive knowledge; along with the amalgamation of theory and practice as the core fundamentals in the advancement of knowledge, the building of an educated society and the development of the university
Mission
To be the learning centre of choice that  promotes the sovereignty of  Bahasa Melayu and internationalises knowledge rooted in the national culture
Educational Goals
Graduates who Appreciate National Aspiration, who are Competent, Competitive and Innovative
TERAS Value
Talent, Ethics, Revitalise, Agile and Soul
Mace
Mace
The Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia (UKM) mace is an endowment from the Seri Paduka Baginda Yang Di-Pertuan Agong with the approval of Majlis Raja-Raja Melayu as the symbol of power for the University. The mace is used in certain formal University events and ceremonies such as the Convocation Ceremony and the Appointments of Chancellors and Pro-Chancellors.
Torch
The torch is located on top of a mosque which signifies knowledge and a strong faith. The torch as a symbol of education signifies the role of the University as an institution of higher learning in contributing knowledge and expertise for the advancement and development of the people.
Vessel
The sphere at the end of the mace is a vessel which signifies the world. This shows that UKM is part of the international knowledge community and ready to embrace all facets of knowledge from all sources and then translates the knowledge for the benefit of the local and international communities. On top of the vessels are the emblems of the states in Malaysia, while on the side of the vessel are the emblems of UKM and Malaysia.
Hibiscus and Five Krises
The vessel which symbolises the world is placed on a hibiscus and supported by five krises, the Malay dagger. The flower is the national flower of Malaysia while the kris reflects the national heritage and traditions. The five krises symbolises the five pillars of Rukun Negara.
Palm Frond
In between the hibiscus and the vessel, there is a carved palm frond (Borassus flabellifer or palmyra), which symbolises literature.
Mace Stem
Along the stem of the mace, there are carvings of various motifs which signify the philosophy of the University and the faculties within. It symbolises the University’s objective to produce dynamic and rounded graduates who are pragmatic and creative and able to contribute towards the advancement of society and the country. In the middle of the stem, paddy grains motifs are artistically fashioned, reflecting the country’s prosperity with rice as the staple food.
Gasing
At the end of the mace is a spinning top with an axis. The five portions of the spinning top represents the five economic sectors of the country; industry, mining, transportation, agriculture and fishery. On the crown of the spinning top, there are carved images of the country’s produce.

Discover UKM
Give2UKM
Bachelor in Computer Science with Honours
Overview
Programme Structure
Computer Science is the scientific and practical approach to computation and its applications. Computer science is a fast-moving field that brings together many disciplines, including mathematics, programming, engineering, the natural sciences, and linguistics. We offer a flexible programme with strong fundamentals of computer science that provides students the required skills to solve problems and to create future technology as a computer scientist. The programme is divided into four (4) tracks Software Technology, Network Technology, Applied Data Science, and Intelligent Machine which the students will be able to choose in their second year. Apart from the 12-weeks industrial training in the fourth year, the programme is designed to be in line with industrial requirements through our industrial partners such as IBM, CISCO, and Skymind.

Bachelor in Information Technology with Honours
Overview
Programme Structure
Students who enrolled in the Information Technology Programme will gain knowledge in information management, web and mobile systems, networking, system administration, user experience design and software fundamentals. They will also be equipped with skills for operations management for industry, enterprise resource planning, internet of things and virtual reality. The career options for graduates in this program range from IT service manager, system administrator, web developer, application programmer and systems analyst. Give2UKM
Bachelor in Software Engineering with Honours
Overview
Programme Structure
Students who enrolled in the Information Systems Development Programme will gain knowledge in software engineering especially in the field of Information Systems Development (the specification, development, implementation, testing, maintenance and evolution of software systems especially for the development of business and organizational-based information systems), web and mobile programming, and software fundamentals. This programme cultivates competent software engineers through strong fundamental knowledge in software development and project management, and hands-on practices of current practical approaches and technologies from the industry, as well as (the students) will be equipped with skills in communication, teamwork, leadership, interpersonal and entrepreneurial mindset. The versatility of our graduates enables them to fit into various career opportunities including software engineers, systems or requirements analysts, software or systems designers, programmers, software testers and information systems.

Bachelor in Software Engineering with Honours (Multimedia System Development)
Overview
Programme Structure
Students who enrolled in the Multimedia Systems Development Programme will gain knowledge in software engineering especially in the field of Multimedia Systems Development (focus on multimedia software production through the analysis, design, development and implementation of the system based on the software engineering principles), web and mobile programming, and software fundamentals. They will also be equipped with skills in communication, teamwork, leadership, interpersonal and entrepreneurial mindset. The career options for graduates in this programme range from multimedia software engineer, multimedia system administrator, games developer, digital media producer and multimedia systems analyst.

Fakulti Teknologi dan Sains Maklumat
Faculty of Information Science and Technology
Discover UKM
Give2UKM
Bachelor in Software Engineering with Honours (Information System Development)
Overview
Programme Structure
Students who enrolled in the Information Systems Development Programme will gain knowledge in software engineering especially in the field of Information Systems Development (the specification, development, implementation, testing, maintenance and evolution of software systems especially for the development of business and organizational-based information systems), web and mobile programming, and software fundamentals. This programme cultivates competent software engineers through strong fundamental knowledge in software development and project management, and hands-on practices of current practical approaches and technologies from the industry, as well as (the students) will be equipped with skills in communication, teamwork, leadership, interpersonal and entrepreneurial mindset. The versatility of our graduates enables them to fit into various career opportunities including software engineers, systems or requirements analysts, software or systems designers, programmers, software testers and information systems.
Mode of Study
Course Work
Duration of Study
Full Time  :  7 - 12 semesters
Part Time  :  7 - 16 semesters

Selamat Datang ke
UKM!
Adakah anda sudah bersedia untuk merasai gabungan pendidikan berkualiti dengan kepelbagaian budaya? Jika jawapan anda “YA”, maka anda telah datang ke tempat yang betul.
Di UKM, pendidikan berkualiti adalah keutamaan ahli profesional kami. Ahli akademik dan staf kami bukan hanya di sini untuk berkongsi pengetahuan mereka, tetapi juga untuk membantu anda merasai keistimewaan pelbagai budaya di kampus kami.
Jadi apa yang anda tunggukan lagi? Marilah mendaftar dengan kami sekarang!

NOTIS PENUTUPAN KAUNTER AKADEMIK UKM
Dimaklumkan bahawa Kaunter AKADEMIK UKM akan ditutup sementara pada ketetapan berikut:
Tarikh: 15 Mei 2026 (Jumaat)
Masa: 10.00 pagi hingga 12.15 tengah hari
Penutupan kaunter ini adalah kerana penglibatan warga AKADEMIK UKM dalam Program Gaya Hidup Sihat.
Sehubungan itu, semua pelajar dimohon untuk merancang urusan berkaitan akademik di luar tempoh penutupan tersebut. Kaunter akan beroperasi semula seperti biasa selepas tamat tempoh program berkenaan.
Segala kesulitan yang timbul amat dikesali dan didahului dengan ucapan terima kasih atas kerjasama serta perhatian semua pelajar.
Sekian, terima kasih.
IKLAN JAWATAN KOSONG PUSAT PENGURUSAN AKADEMIK (AKADEMIK UKM)
Sukacita dimaklumkan bahawa Pusat Pengurusan Akademik (AKADEMIK UKM), Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia (UKM) mempelawa Warganegara Malaysia yang berkelayakan, komited dan berdedikasi untuk berkhidmat di organisasi kami bagi jawatan seperti berikut:
Pegawai Teknologi Maklumat (Gred F9) secara kontrak;
Penolong Pegawai Teknologi Maklumat (Gred FA5) secara kontrak.
Penempatan:
Pusat Pengurusan Akademik (AKADEMIK UKM)
Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia
Calon yang berminat dan berkelayakan, sila lengkapkan maklumat diri dan hantarkan resume/CV terkini serta salinan sijil akademik melalui pautan:
https://forms.gle/GKXK2DoUnj8e3Y9s9
Sebarang pertanyaan, sila hubungi kami di talian 03-89118024 atau e-mel kepada tpakad@ukm.edu.my. Hanya calon-calon yang layak selepas tapisan sahaja akan dipanggil menghadiri sesi temubual. Sekiranya calon tidak menerima sebarang maklumat selepas satu (1) bulan) dari tarikh tutup permohonan, calon dianggap gagal melepasi saringan pertama.
Tarikh tutup permohonan:
3 FEBRUARI 2026 (SELASA)
Sekian, terima kasih.
PUSAT PENGURUSAN AKADEMIK
